It manages the complete lifecycle of calculations: input generation, remote HPC job submission via SSH, output retrieval and parsing, automatic error handling and restart, and immutable provenance recording in a directed acycli ([SFETNI/Deep-Matter-Chem-Skills](https://github.com/SFETNI/Deep-Matter-Chem-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/electronic-structure/aiida-workflow) · ★11)
- **aiinbx-cli**: Operate AI Inbx from the terminal with the `aiinbx` CLI — send email (files, stdin, React Email templates), read threads and emails, manage domains, spaces, assigned addresses, mailboxes, webhooks, suppressions and every other API resource; forward webhook events to localhost with `aiinbx listen`; block until an email arrives with `aiinbx wait` (OTP / verification flows, e2e tests); check DNS and setup with `aiinbx doctor`. Load this skill before running any `aiinbx` command: it holds the non-interactive contract, JSON output and exit codes. ([aiinbx/skills](https://github.com/aiinbx/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/aiinbx-cli))
- **aiinbx-concepts**: Understanding AI Inbx's objects before acting on them — what makes an address one the workspace can send from (a domain or a connected mailbox), how an email relates to its thread, what a send response and an event each prove, why a recipient can be dropped or a message held, and how spaces keep each customer's mail apart, by domain or by single address. Load before designing or changing an AI Inbx integration, or when an email, thread or event does not behave as expected. ([aiinbx/skills](https://github.com/aiinbx/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/aiinbx-concepts))
- **aiinbx-integrate**: Integrating AI Inbx into an app's server code with the TypeScript or Python SDK — sending email, receiving it through a verified webhook, reading threads, replying in a thread, drafts a person reviews, and acting on bounces and other delivery events. Use when the user asks to send email from their app, handle inbound email or replies, build an email agent, or change an existing AI Inbx integration. Needs an API key in the project's environment; use aiinbx-setup first if the project is not connected yet. ([aiinbx/skills](https://github.com/aiinbx/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/aiinbx-integrate))
- **aiinbx-setup**: First-time AI Inbx setup — install the `aiinbx` CLI, sign in, put an API key in the project's environment, and get the app sending an email and answering the reply in the same thread, verified end to end. Use when the user is new to AI Inbx, pastes an AI Inbx setup prompt, or asks to add email to their app: sending, receiving, inbound email, replies, threads, an email agent. If AI Inbx is already set up in the project, use aiinbx-integrate instead. ([aiinbx/skills](https://github.com/aiinbx/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/aiinbx-setup))
- **aiinsight-shorts**: Make narrated faceless YouTube Shorts / TikToks / Reels fully in code on the user's computer, with a free local AI voice and word-synced captions, $0. Six styles - Deep Dive 3D (3D "what happens if" body/science explainers), Night File 3D (unsolved mysteries, true crime), Inside Out (flat 2D x-ray explainers), Stick Story (stick-figure "what would you do if" survival steps), Paper Atlas (history in cut paper with maps), Tall Tale 3D (funny true stories with flat 2D cartoon people in a sunny 3D world, one-word captions). Use whenever someone wants a faceless short, a what-if or what-happens-if… ([AMG555/FrameForge](https://github.com/AMG555/FrameForge/tree/HEAD/plugins/aiinsight-shorts))
